  as-2e as-2f as-2g as-2h as-3o as-44 as-45 as-6t ⚙1vp2hox"><path d="M8.75 1a.75.75 0 0 0-1.5 0v8.69L5.03 7.47a.75.75 0 0 0-1.06 1.06l3.5 3.5a.75.75 0 0 0 1.06 0l3.5-3.5a.75.75 0 0 0-1.06-1.06L8.75 tools</span></button></div></div></div><div class="Document" data-testid="docs-content"><p>Subscriptions let customers make recurring payments to access a product or service. When you create a subscription, Stripe automatically generates invoices, attempts payment collection, and manages the subscription status throughout its lifecycle. A subscription moves through a predictable set of states, from creation to cancellation.</p><p>Unlike one-time payments, subscriptions require storing customer and payment method information for future billing cycles. Stripe handles the payment retry logic, dunning, and status transitions.</p><h2 class="Heading Heading--anchored" id="subscription-lifecycle" data-toc-text="Subscription lifecycle"><span class="⚙ rs-4 as-w as-y as-1r ⚙w54g2d"><span class="⚙ rs-4  as-w as-y as-1r as-v ⚙1hdidop"><span class="algolia-heading-content ⚙">Subscription lifecycle </span></span><span class="⚙ as-3r ⚙1kc9vkf"><span role="button" class="⚙  as-3r as-1e as-1u as-1v as-6v ⚙sm5kdb"><svg data-testid="anchorIcon" aria-hidden="true" width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 16 16" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" class="⚙   as-2e as-2f as-2g as-2h as-2i as-44 as-45 as-6w as-6x ⚙1o8zlja"><path d="M12.707 7.091a2.685 2.685 0 0 0-3.798-3.798L7.763 4.44a.753.753 0 1 1-1.066-1.066l1.147-1.146a4.192 4.192 0 0 1 5.928 5.928l-1.146 1.147a.753.753 0 0 1-1.066-1.066l1.147-1.146Z"></path><path d="M5.26 10.76a.753.753 0 0 1 0-1.066l4.434-4.435a.753.753 0 0 1 1.065 1.065L6.325 10.76a.753.753 0 0 1-1.066 0Z"></path><path d="M3.293 12.707a2.685 2.685 0 0 1 0-3.798L4.44 7.763a.753.753 0 1 0-1.066-1.066L2.228 7.844a4.192 4.192 0 0 0 5.928 5.928l1.147-1.146a.753.753 0 0 0-1.066-1.066l-1.146 1.147a2.685 2.685 0 0 1-3.798 0Z"></path></svg></span></span></span></h2><p>Each of the following subscription lifecycle phases maps to a status change on the <a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/api/subscriptions/object" tabindex="0"><span>Subscription object</span></a>. Understanding these statuses helps you know when to provision access, notify customers, and handle errors. You can use <span><span><a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/billing/subscriptions/webhooks#state-changes" tabindex="0"><span>webhook events</span></a></span><span class="⚙  as-d as-6y as-6z as-h as-70 ⚙1rjyh41"></span></span> to monitor and handle transitions between statuses.</p><h3 class="Heading Heading--anchored" id="create-the-subscription" data-toc-text="Create the subscription"><span class="⚙ rs-4 as-w as-y as-1r ⚙w54g2d"><span class="⚙ rs-4  as-w as-y as-1r as-v ⚙1hdidop"><span class="algolia-heading-content ⚙">Create the subscription</span></span><span class="⚙ as-3r ⚙1kc9vkf"><span role="button" class="⚙  as-3r as-1e as-1u as-1v as-6v ⚙sm5kdb"><svg data-testid="anchorIcon" aria-hidden="true" width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 16 16" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" class="⚙   as-2e as-2f as-2g as-2h as-2i as-44 as-45 as-6w as-6x ⚙1o8zlja"><path d="M12.707 7.091a2.685 2.685 0 0 0-3.798-3.798L7.763 4.44a.753.753 0 1 1-1.066-1.066l1.147-1.146a4.192 4.192 0 0 1 5.928 5.928l-1.146 1.147a.753.753 0 0 1-1.066-1.066l1.147-1.146Z"></path><path d="M5.26 10.76a.753.753 0 0 1 0-1.066l4.434-4.435a.753.753 0 0 1 1.065 1.065L6.325 10.76a.753.753 0 0 1-1.066 0Z"></path><path d="M3.293 12.707a2.685 2.685 0 0 1 0-3.798L4.44 7.763a.753.753 0 1 0-1.066-1.066L2.228 7.844a4.192 4.192 0 0 0 5.928 5.928l1.147-1.146a.753.753 0 0 0-1.066-1.066l-1.146 1.147a2.685 2.685 0 0 1-3.798 0Z"></path></svg></span></span></span></h3><p>Create a new subscription in the <a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="https://dashboard.stripe.com/subscriptions?status=active" tabindex="0"><span>Dashboard</span></a> or with the <a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/api/subscriptions/create" tabindex="0"><span>Subscriptions API</span></a>. The resulting <a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/api/subscriptions/object" tabindex="0"><span>Subscription object</span></a> contains the subscribed customer, <a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/api/products" tabindex="0"><span>product</span></a>, and <a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/api/prices/object" tabindex="0"><span>price</span></a>, and the <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">status</code> reflecting the subscription’s current lifecycle state.</p><p>When you create a subscription that requires an immediate payment, Stripe also creates an <span><span><a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/billing/invoices/subscription" tabindex="0"><span>Invoice</span></a></span><span class="⚙  as-d as-6y as-6z as-h as-70 ⚙1rjyh41"></span></span> and a <span><span><a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/payments/payment-intents" tabindex="0"><span>PaymentIntent</span></a></span><span class="⚙  as-d as-6y as-6z as-h as-70 ⚙1rjyh41"></span></span>. The subscription’s initial status is <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">incomplete</code>, then becomes <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">active</code> after the customer pays the first invoice.</p><div class="CalloutWrapper ⚙ rs-4  as-73 as-74 as-u as-1r as-3x as-75 as-76 as-77 ⚙13c6dd2"><h4 data-component="CalloutHeading" class="⚙ rs-3 as-78 as-79 as-16 as-7a as-2r ⚙4pf0e0">Note</h4><div class="⚙ rs-3 as-4b ⚙1oqxyif"><p>Payment methods with <span><span><a class="UnstyledLink InlineLink Text-color--blue Glossary-term no-api-tag" href="/payments/payment-methods">delayed</a></span><span class="⚙  as-d as-6y as-6z as-h as-70 ⚙1rjyh41"></span></span> payment confirmation timing, such as ACH Direct Debit, might have a different status. For more details, see <span><span><a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="#delayed-payment-confirmation" tabindex="0"><span>Delayed payment confirmation</span></a></span><span class="⚙  as-d as-6y as-6z as-h as-70 ⚙1rjyh41"></span></span>.</p></div></div><p>Default subscription payment collection and failure handling depends on the payment method. In the API, you can configure a subscription’s <a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/api/subscriptions/create#create_subscription-payment_behavior" tabindex="0"><span>payment_behavior</span></a> to override the default.</p><p>If you create a <span><span><a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/billing/subscriptions/trials" tabindex="0"><span>trial period</span></a></span><span class="⚙  as-d as-6y as-6z as-h as-70 ⚙1rjyh41"></span></span> to delay the first charge for the subscription, the initial status is <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">trialing</code>, and the subscription automatically transitions to <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">active</code> when the trial ends and payment succeeds.</p><h3 class="Heading Heading--anchored" id="handle-the-invoice" data-toc-text="Handle the invoice"><span class="⚙ rs-4 as-w as-y as-1r ⚙w54g2d"><span class="⚙ rs-4  as-w as-y as-1r as-v ⚙1hdidop"><span class="algolia-heading-content ⚙">Handle the invoice</span></span><span class="⚙ as-3r ⚙1kc9vkf"><span role="button" class="⚙  as-3r as-1e as-1u as-1v as-6v ⚙sm5kdb"><svg data-testid="anchorIcon" aria-hidden="true" width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 16 16" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" class="⚙   as-2e as-2f as-2g as-2h as-2i as-44 as-45 as-6w as-6x ⚙1o8zlja"><path d="M12.707 7.091a2.685 2.685 0 0 0-3.798-3.798L7.763 4.44a.753.753 0 1 1-1.066-1.066l1.147-1.146a4.192 4.192 0 0 1 5.928 5.928l-1.146 1.147a.753.753 0 0 1-1.066-1.066l1.147-1.146Z"></path><path d="M5.26 10.76a.753.753 0 0 1 0-1.066l4.434-4.435a.753.753 0 0 1 1.065 1.065L6.325 10.76a.753.753 0 0 1-1.066 0Z"></path><path d="M3.293 12.707a2.685 2.685 0 0 1 0-3.798L4.44 7.763a.753.753 0 1 0-1.066-1.066L2.228 7.844a4.192 4.192 0 0 0 5.928 5.928l1.147-1.146a.753.753 0 0 0-1.066-1.066l-1.146 1.147a2.685 2.685 0 0 1-3.798 0Z"></path></svg></span></span></span></h3><p>For subscriptions with <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">collection<!-- -->_<wbr/>method</code> set to <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">charge<!-- -->_<wbr/>automatically</code>, Stripe creates an <span><span><a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/billing/invoices/subscription" tabindex="0"><span>invoice</span></a></span><span class="⚙  as-d as-6y as-6z as-h as-70 ⚙1rjyh41"></span></span> with the status <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">open</code> when you create the subscription. Your customer has 23 hours to pay. During this time, the subscription status is <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">incomplete</code> and the invoice status remains <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">open</code>. This 23-hour window accommodates customers who pay while <span><span><span tabindex="0" class="Glossary-term Text-color--blue Text-fontWeight--medium Text-numericSpacing--proportional Text-typeface--base Text-wrap--wrap Text-display--inline">on-session</span></span><span class="⚙  as-d as-6y as-6z as-h as-70 ⚙1rjyh41"></span></span>. If the customer returns to your application after 23 hours, create a new subscription for them.</p><p>For subscriptions with <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">collection<!-- -->_<wbr/>method</code> set to <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">send<!-- -->_<wbr/>invoice</code>, Stripe emails the customer a link to the invoice with a configurable due date. The subscription remains <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">incomplete</code> until the customer pays.</p><p>To learn more, see <span><span><a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/billing/invoices/subscription" tabindex="0"><span>Subscription invoices</span></a></span><span class="⚙  as-d as-6y as-6z as-h as-70 ⚙1rjyh41"></span></span>.</p><h3 class="Heading Heading--anchored" id="confirm-payment" data-toc-text="Confirm payment"><span class="⚙ rs-4 as-w as-y as-1r ⚙w54g2d"><span class="⚙ rs-4  as-w as-y as-1r as-v ⚙1hdidop"><span class="algolia-heading-content ⚙">Confirm payment</span></span><span class="⚙ as-3r ⚙1kc9vkf"><span role="button" class="⚙  as-3r as-1e as-1u as-1v as-6v ⚙sm5kdb"><svg data-testid="anchorIcon" aria-hidden="true" width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 16 16" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" class="⚙   as-2e as-2f as-2g as-2h as-2i as-44 as-45 as-6w as-6x ⚙1o8zlja"><path d="M12.707 7.091a2.685 2.685 0 0 0-3.798-3.798L7.763 4.44a.753.753 0 1 1-1.066-1.066l1.147-1.146a4.192 4.192 0 0 1 5.928 5.928l-1.146 1.147a.753.753 0 0 1-1.066-1.066l1.147-1.146Z"></path><path d="M5.26 10.76a.753.753 0 0 1 0-1.066l4.434-4.435a.753.753 0 0 1 1.065 1.065L6.325 10.76a.753.753 0 0 1-1.066 0Z"></path><path d="M3.293 12.707a2.685 2.685 0 0 1 0-3.798L4.44 7.763a.753.753 0 1 0-1.066-1.066L2.228 7.844a4.192 4.192 0 0 0 5.928 5.928l1.147-1.146a.753.753 0 0 0-1.066-1.066l-1.146 1.147a2.685 2.685 0 0 1-3.798 0Z"></path></svg></span></span></span></h3><p>If your customer pays the invoice, the subscription updates to <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">active</code> and the invoice to <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">paid</code>. Listen for the <span><span><a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/billing/subscriptions/webhooks#events" tabindex="0"><span>invoice.paid</span></a></span><span class="⚙  as-d as-6y as-6z as-h as-70 ⚙1rjyh41"></span></span> event or confirm that the subscription status is <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">active</code>.</p><p>If the customer doesn’t pay within 23 hours, the subscription updates to <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">incomplete<!-- -->_<wbr/>expired</code> and the invoice becomes <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">void</code>. To reactivate their access, create a new subscription.</p><p>For more details, see <span><span><a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="#subscription-statuses" tabindex="0"><span>Subscription statuses</span></a></span><span class="⚙  as-d as-6y as-6z as-h as-70 ⚙1rjyh41"></span></span> and <span><span><a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="#payment-status" tabindex="0"><span>Payment statuses</span></a></span><span class="⚙  as-d as-6y as-6z as-h as-70 ⚙1rjyh41"></span></span>.</p><h3 class="Heading Heading--anchored" id="provision-access" data-toc-text="Provision access to your product"><span class="⚙ rs-4 as-w as-y as-1r ⚙w54g2d"><span class="⚙ rs-4  as-w as-y as-1r as-v ⚙1hdidop"><span class="algolia-heading-content ⚙">Provision access to your product </span></span><span class="⚙ as-3r ⚙1kc9vkf"><span role="button" class="⚙  as-3r as-1e as-1u as-1v as-6v ⚙sm5kdb"><svg data-testid="anchorIcon" aria-hidden="true" width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 16 16" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" class="⚙   as-2e as-2f as-2g as-2h as-2i as-44 as-45 as-6w as-6x ⚙1o8zlja"><path d="M12.707 7.091a2.685 2.685 0 0 0-3.798-3.798L7.763 4.44a.753.753 0 1 1-1.066-1.066l1.147-1.146a4.192 4.192 0 0 1 5.928 5.928l-1.146 1.147a.753.753 0 0 1-1.066-1.066l1.147-1.146Z"></path><path d="M5.26 10.76a.753.753 0 0 1 0-1.066l4.434-4.435a.753.753 0 0 1 1.065 1.065L6.325 10.76a.753.753 0 0 1-1.066 0Z"></path><path d="M3.293 12.707a2.685 2.685 0 0 1 0-3.798L4.44 7.763a.753.753 0 1 0-1.066-1.066L2.228 7.844a4.192 4.192 0 0 0 5.928 5.928l1.147-1.146a.753.753 0 0 0-1.066-1.066l-1.146 1.147a2.685 2.685 0 0 1-3.798 0Z"></path></svg></span></span></span></h3><p>When a subscription becomes <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">active</code>, Stripe creates an active <span><span><a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/billing/entitlements" tabindex="0"><span>entitlement</span></a></span><span class="⚙  as-d as-6y as-6z as-h as-70 ⚙1rjyh41"></span></span> for each feature associated with the subscribed product. When a customer accesses your services, use their active entitlements to grant them access to the features included in their subscription.</p><p>Alternatively, <span><span><a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/billing/subscriptions/webhooks#active-subscriptions" tabindex="0"><span>track active subscriptions</span></a></span><span class="⚙  as-d as-6y as-6z as-h as-70 ⚙1rjyh41"></span></span> with webhook events and provision the product for the customer based on that activity.</p><h3 class="Heading Heading--anchored" id="update-the-subscription" data-toc-text="Update the subscription"><span class="⚙ rs-4 as-w as-y as-1r ⚙w54g2d"><span class="⚙ rs-4  as-w as-y as-1r as-v ⚙1hdidop"><span class="algolia-heading-content ⚙">Update the subscription</span></span><span class="⚙ as-3r ⚙1kc9vkf"><span role="button" class="⚙  as-3r as-1e as-1u as-1v as-6v ⚙sm5kdb"><svg data-testid="anchorIcon" aria-hidden="true" width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 16 16" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" class="⚙   as-2e as-2f as-2g as-2h as-2i as-44 as-45 as-6w as-6x ⚙1o8zlja"><path d="M12.707 7.091a2.685 2.685 0 0 0-3.798-3.798L7.763 4.44a.753.753 0 1 1-1.066-1.066l1.147-1.146a4.192 4.192 0 0 1 5.928 5.928l-1.146 1.147a.753.753 0 0 1-1.066-1.066l1.147-1.146Z"></path><path d="M5.26 10.76a.753.753 0 0 1 0-1.066l4.434-4.435a.753.753 0 0 1 1.065 1.065L6.325 10.76a.753.753 0 0 1-1.066 0Z"></path><path d="M3.293 12.707a2.685 2.685 0 0 1 0-3.798L4.44 7.763a.753.753 0 1 0-1.066-1.066L2.228 7.844a4.192 4.192 0 0 0 5.928 5.928l1.147-1.146a.753.753 0 0 0-1.066-1.066l-1.146 1.147a2.685 2.685 0 0 1-3.798 0Z"></path></svg></span></span></span></h3><p>You can <span><span><a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/billing/subscriptions/change" tabindex="0"><span>modify existing subscriptions</span></a></span><span class="⚙  as-d as-6y as-6z as-h as-70 ⚙1rjyh41"></span></span> as needed without having to cancel and recreate them. Some of the most significant changes you might make are <span><span><a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/billing/subscriptions/change-price" tabindex="0"><span>upgrading or downgrading</span></a></span><span class="⚙  as-d as-6y as-6z as-h as-70 ⚙1rjyh41"></span></span> the subscription price or <span><span><a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/billing/subscriptions/pause-payment" tabindex="0"><span>pausing payment collection</span></a></span><span class="⚙  as-d as-6y as-6z as-h as-70 ⚙1rjyh41"></span></span> for an active subscription.</p><p>For <span><span><a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/payments/checkout" tabindex="0"><span>Stripe Checkout</span></a></span><span class="⚙  as-d as-6y as-6z as-h as-70 ⚙1rjyh41"></span></span> integrations, you can’t update the subscription or its invoice if the session’s subscription is <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">incomplete</code>. You can listen to the <a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/api/events/types#event_types-checkout.session.completed" tabindex="0"><span>checkout.session.completed</span></a> event to make the update after the session has completed. You can also <a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/api/checkout/sessions/expire" tabindex="0"><span>expire the session</span></a> instead if you want to cancel the session’s subscription, void the subscription invoice, or mark the invoice as uncollectible.</p><h3 class="Heading Heading--anchored" id="unpaid-subscriptions" data-toc-text="Handle unpaid subscriptions"><span class="⚙ rs-4 as-w as-y as-1r ⚙w54g2d"><span class="⚙ rs-4  as-w as-y as-1r as-v ⚙1hdidop"><span class="algolia-heading-content ⚙">Handle unpaid subscriptions </span></span><span class="⚙ as-3r ⚙1kc9vkf"><span role="button" class="⚙  as-3r as-1e as-1u as-1v as-6v ⚙sm5kdb"><svg data-testid="anchorIcon" aria-hidden="true" width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 16 16" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" class="⚙   as-2e as-2f as-2g as-2h as-2i as-44 as-45 as-6w as-6x ⚙1o8zlja"><path d="M12.707 7.091a2.685 2.685 0 0 0-3.798-3.798L7.763 4.44a.753.753 0 1 1-1.066-1.066l1.147-1.146a4.192 4.192 0 0 1 5.928 5.928l-1.146 1.147a.753.753 0 0 1-1.066-1.066l1.147-1.146Z"></path><path d="M5.26 10.76a.753.753 0 0 1 0-1.066l4.434-4.435a.753.753 0 0 1 1.065 1.065L6.325 10.76a.753.753 0 0 1-1.066 0Z"></path><path d="M3.293 12.707a2.685 2.685 0 0 1 0-3.798L4.44 7.763a.753.753 0 1 0-1.066-1.066L2.228 7.844a4.192 4.192 0 0 0 5.928 5.928l1.147-1.146a.753.753 0 0 0-1.066-1.066l-1.146 1.147a2.685 2.685 0 0 1-3.798 0Z"></path></svg></span></span></span></h3><p>If the customer doesn’t pay a subscription invoice, Stripe pauses further collection attempts. The subscription continues to generate invoices each billing period, which remain in <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">draft</code> status. The subscription’s status (<code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">past<!-- -->_<wbr/>due</code> or <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">unpaid</code>) depends on your <a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="https://dashboard.stripe.com/settings/billing/automatic#manage-failed-payments" tabindex="0"><span>failed payment settings</span></a> in the Dashboard.</p><p>Voided invoices don’t affect subscription status. Stripe determines the status from the most recent non-voided invoice. To learn more, see <span><span><a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/billing/collection-method#failed-subscription-payments" tabindex="0"><span>Failed subscription payments</span></a></span><span class="⚙  as-d as-6y as-6z as-h as-70 ⚙1rjyh41"></span></span>.</p><h3 class="Heading Heading--anchored" id="cancel-subscriptions" data-toc-text="Cancel the subscription"><span class="⚙ rs-4 as-w as-y as-1r ⚙w54g2d"><span class="⚙ rs-4  as-w as-y as-1r as-v ⚙1hdidop"><span class="algolia-heading-content ⚙">Cancel the subscription </span></span><span class="⚙ as-3r ⚙1kc9vkf"><span role="button" class="⚙  as-3r as-1e as-1u as-1v as-6v ⚙sm5kdb"><svg data-testid="anchorIcon" aria-hidden="true" width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 16 16" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" class="⚙   as-2e as-2f as-2g as-2h as-2i as-44 as-45 as-6w as-6x ⚙1o8zlja"><path d="M12.707 7.091a2.685 2.685 0 0 0-3.798-3.798L7.763 4.44a.753.753 0 1 1-1.066-1.066l1.147-1.146a4.192 4.192 0 0 1 5.928 5.928l-1.146 1.147a.753.753 0 0 1-1.066-1.066l1.147-1.146Z"></path><path d="M5.26 10.76a.753.753 0 0 1 0-1.066l4.434-4.435a.753.753 0 0 1 1.065 1.065L6.325 10.76a.753.753 0 0 1-1.066 0Z"></path><path d="M3.293 12.707a2.685 2.685 0 0 1 0-3.798L4.44 7.763a.753.753 0 1 0-1.066-1.066L2.228 7.844a4.192 4.192 0 0 0 5.928 5.928l1.147-1.146a.753.753 0 0 0-1.066-1.066l-1.146 1.147a2.685 2.685 0 0 1-3.798 0Z"></path></svg></span></span></span></h3><p>You can <span><span><a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/billing/subscriptions/cancel" tabindex="0"><span>cancel</span></a></span><span class="⚙  as-d as-6y as-6z as-h as-70 ⚙1rjyh41"></span></span> a subscription at any time, including at the <span><span><a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/billing/subscriptions/cancel#cancel-at-the-end-of-the-current-billing-period" tabindex="0"><span>end of a billing cycle</span></a></span><span class="⚙  as-d as-6y as-6z as-h as-70 ⚙1rjyh41"></span></span> or after a <span><span><a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/billing/subscriptions/cancel#subscription-schedules" tabindex="0"><span>set number of billing cycles</span></a></span><span class="⚙  as-d as-6y as-6z as-h as-70 ⚙1rjyh41"></span></span>.</p><p>By default, canceling a subscription disables creating new invoices and <span><span><a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/billing/subscriptions/cancel#handle-invoice-items-when-canceling-subscriptions" tabindex="0"><span>stops automatic collection</span></a></span><span class="⚙  as-d as-6y as-6z as-h as-70 ⚙1rjyh41"></span></span> of all outstanding invoices from the subscription. It also deletes the subscription and you can no longer update it except for its <span><span><a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/metadata" tabindex="0"><span>metadata</span></a></span><span class="⚙  as-d as-6y as-6z as-h as-70 ⚙1rjyh41"></span></span> and <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">cancellation<!-- -->_<wbr/>details</code>. If your customer wants to resubscribe, you need to collect new payment information from them and create a new subscription.</p><h2 class="Heading Heading--anchored" id="subscription-statuses" data-toc-text="Subscription statuses"><span class="⚙ rs-4 as-w as-y as-1r ⚙w54g2d"><span class="⚙ rs-4  as-w as-y as-1r as-v ⚙1hdidop"><span class="algolia-heading-content ⚙">Subscription statuses </span></span><span class="⚙ as-3r ⚙1kc9vkf"><span role="button" class="⚙  as-3r as-1e as-1u as-1v as-6v ⚙sm5kdb"><svg data-testid="anchorIcon" aria-hidden="true" width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 16 16" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" class="⚙   as-2e as-2f as-2g as-2h as-2i as-44 as-45 as-6w as-6x ⚙1o8zlja"><path d="M12.707 7.091a2.685 2.685 0 0 0-3.798-3.798L7.763 4.44a.753.753 0 1 1-1.066-1.066l1.147-1.146a4.192 4.192 0 0 1 5.928 5.928l-1.146 1.147a.753.753 0 0 1-1.066-1.066l1.147-1.146Z"></path><path d="M5.26 10.76a.753.753 0 0 1 0-1.066l4.434-4.435a.753.753 0 0 1 1.065 1.065L6.325 10.76a.753.753 0 0 1-1.066 0Z"></path><path d="M3.293 12.707a2.685 2.685 0 0 1 0-3.798L4.44 7.763a.753.753 0 1 0-1.066-1.066L2.228 7.844a4.192 4.192 0 0 0 5.928 5.928l1.147-1.146a.753.753 0 0 0-1.066-1.066l-1.146 1.147a2.685 2.685 0 0 1-3.798 0Z"></path></svg></span></span></span></h2><p>Subscriptions can have the following statuses. The actions you can take on a subscription depend on its status.</p><div class="Table Table--striped ⚙  as-3r as-4r as-6o as-x ⚙1yz2rkt"><div class="⚙  as-7b as-7c as-7d as-7e as-7f as-7g as-7h ⚙ld3g7k"><table class="⚙  as-x as-7i as-7j as-5o as-53 as-7k as-7l as-7m as-7n ⚙by0pj1"><thead><tr class="⚙ as-2z ⚙lu93gh"><th class="⚙ rs-3 as-7o as-7p as-7q as-12 as-7r as-3n as-3o as-54 as-7s ⚙batyg0" scope="column">Status</th><th class="⚙ rs-3 as-7o as-7p as-7q as-12 as-7r as-3n as-3o as-54 as-7s ⚙batyg0" scope="column">Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr class="⚙ as-2z ⚙lu93gh"><td class="⚙  as-7p as-7t as-l as-7u as-4n as-7v as-7w as-7x ⚙1gceb7q"><code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">trialing</code></td><td class="⚙  as-7p as-7t as-l as-7u as-4n as-7v as-7w as-7x ⚙1gceb7q">The subscription is currently in a trial period and you can safely provision your product for your customer. The subscription transitions automatically to <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">active</code> when a customer makes the first payment.</td></tr><tr class="⚙ as-2z ⚙lu93gh"><td class="⚙  as-7p as-7t as-l as-7u as-4n as-7v as-7w as-7x ⚙1gceb7q"><p><code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">active</code></p></td><td class="⚙  as-7p as-7t as-l as-7u as-4n as-7v as-7w as-7x ⚙1gceb7q"><p>The subscription is in good standing. For <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">past<!-- -->_<wbr/>due</code> subscriptions, paying the latest associated invoice or marking it uncollectible transitions the subscription to <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">active</code>.</p><p><code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">active</code> doesn’t indicate that all outstanding invoices associated with the subscription have been paid. You can leave other outstanding invoices open for payment, mark them as uncollectible, or void them as you see fit.</p></td></tr><tr class="⚙ as-2z ⚙lu93gh"><td class="⚙  as-7p as-7t as-l as-7u as-4n as-7v as-7w as-7x ⚙1gceb7q"><code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">incomplete</code></td><td class="⚙  as-7p as-7t as-l as-7u as-4n as-7v as-7w as-7x ⚙1gceb7q">The customer must make a successful payment within 23 hours to activate the subscription. Or the payment <span><span><a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/billing/subscriptions/overview#requires-action" tabindex="0"><span>requires action</span></a></span><span class="⚙  as-d as-6y as-6z as-h as-70 ⚙1rjyh41"></span></span>, such as customer authentication. Subscriptions can also be <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">incomplete</code> if there’s a pending payment and the PaymentIntent status is <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">processing</code>.</td></tr><tr class="⚙ as-2z ⚙lu93gh"><td class="⚙  as-7p as-7t as-l as-7u as-4n as-7v as-7w as-7x ⚙1gceb7q"><code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">incomplete<!-- -->_<wbr/>expired</code></td><td class="⚙  as-7p as-7t as-l as-7u as-4n as-7v as-7w as-7x ⚙1gceb7q">The initial payment on the subscription failed and the customer didn’t make a successful payment within 23 hours of subscription creation. These subscriptions don’t bill customers. This status exists so you can track customers that failed to activate their subscriptions.</td></tr><tr class="⚙ as-2z ⚙lu93gh"><td class="⚙  as-7p as-7t as-l as-7u as-4n as-7v as-7w as-7x ⚙1gceb7q"><p><code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">past<!-- -->_<wbr/>due</code></p></td><td class="⚙  as-7p as-7t as-l as-7u as-4n as-7v as-7w as-7x ⚙1gceb7q"><p>Payment on the latest <em>finalized</em> invoice either failed or wasn’t attempted. The subscription continues to create invoices. Your Dashboard <a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="https://dashboard.stripe.com/settings/billing/automatic" tabindex="0"><span>subscription settings</span></a> determine the subscription’s next status. If the invoice is still unpaid after all attempted <span><span><a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/billing/revenue-recovery/smart-retries" tabindex="0"><span>smart retries</span></a></span><span class="⚙  as-d as-6y as-6z as-h as-70 ⚙1rjyh41"></span></span>, you can configure the subscription to move to <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">canceled</code>, <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">unpaid</code>, or leave it as <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">past<!-- -->_<wbr/>due</code>.</p><p>To reactivate the subscription, have your customer pay the most recent invoice. The subscription status becomes <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">active</code> regardless of whether the payment is done before or after the latest invoice due date.</p></td></tr><tr class="⚙ as-2z ⚙lu93gh"><td class="⚙  as-7p as-7t as-l as-7u as-4n as-7v as-7w as-7x ⚙1gceb7q"><code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">canceled</code></td><td class="⚙  as-7p as-7t as-l as-7u as-4n as-7v as-7w as-7x ⚙1gceb7q">The subscription was canceled. During cancellation, automatic collection for all unpaid invoices is disabled (<code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">auto<!-- -->_<wbr/>advance=false</code>). This is a terminal state that can’t be updated.</td></tr><tr class="⚙ as-2z ⚙lu93gh"><td class="⚙  as-7p as-7t as-l as-7u as-4n as-7v as-7w as-7x ⚙1gceb7q"><code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">unpaid</code></td><td class="⚙  as-7p as-7t as-l as-7u as-4n as-7v as-7w as-7x ⚙1gceb7q">The latest invoice hasn’t been paid but the subscription remains in place. The latest invoice remains open and invoices continue to generate, but payments aren’t attempted. Revoke access to your product when the subscription is <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">unpaid</code> because payments were already attempted and retried while <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">past<!-- -->_<wbr/>due</code>. To move the subscription to <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">active</code>, pay the most recent invoice before its due date.</td></tr><tr class="⚙ as-2z ⚙lu93gh"><td class="⚙  as-7p as-7t as-l as-7u as-4n as-7v as-7w as-7x ⚙1gceb7q"><code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">paused</code></td><td class="⚙  as-7p as-7t as-l as-7u as-4n as-7v as-7w as-7x ⚙1gceb7q">The subscription has ended its trial period without a default payment method and the <span><span><a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/billing/subscriptions/trials/free-trials#create-free-trials-without-payment" tabindex="0"><span>trial_settings.end_behavior.missing_payment_method</span></a></span><span class="⚙  as-d as-6y as-6z as-h as-70 ⚙1rjyh41"></span></span> is set to <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">pause</code>. Invoices are no longer created for the subscription. After attaching a default payment method to the customer, you can <span><span><a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/billing/subscriptions/trials/free-trials#resume-a-paused-subscription" tabindex="0"><span>resume the subscription</span></a></span><span class="⚙  as-d as-6y as-6z as-h as-70 ⚙1rjyh41"></span></span>.</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div><h2 class="Heading Heading--anchored" id="payment-status" data-toc-text="Payment statuses"><span class="⚙ rs-4 as-w as-y as-1r ⚙w54g2d"><span class="⚙ rs-4  as-w as-y as-1r as-v ⚙1hdidop"><span class="algolia-heading-content ⚙">Payment statuses </span></span><span class="⚙ as-3r ⚙1kc9vkf"><span role="button" class="⚙  as-3r as-1e as-1u as-1v as-6v ⚙sm5kdb"><svg data-testid="anchorIcon" aria-hidden="true" width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 16 16" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" class="⚙   as-2e as-2f as-2g as-2h as-2i as-44 as-45 as-6w as-6x ⚙1o8zlja"><path d="M12.707 7.091a2.685 2.685 0 0 0-3.798-3.798L7.763 4.44a.753.753 0 1 1-1.066-1.066l1.147-1.146a4.192 4.192 0 0 1 5.928 5.928l-1.146 1.147a.753.753 0 0 1-1.066-1.066l1.147-1.146Z"></path><path d="M5.26 10.76a.753.753 0 0 1 0-1.066l4.434-4.435a.753.753 0 0 1 1.065 1.065L6.325 10.76a.753.753 0 0 1-1.066 0Z"></path><path d="M3.293 12.707a2.685 2.685 0 0 1 0-3.798L4.44 7.763a.753.753 0 1 0-1.066-1.066L2.228 7.844a4.192 4.192 0 0 0 5.928 5.928l1.147-1.146a.753.753 0 0 0-1.066-1.066l-1.146 1.147a2.685 2.685 0 0 1-3.798 0Z"></path></svg></span></span></span></h2><p>A <span><span><a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/payments/payment-intents" tabindex="0"><span>PaymentIntent</span></a></span><span class="⚙  as-d as-6y as-6z as-h as-70 ⚙1rjyh41"></span></span> tracks the lifecycle of every payment. Whenever a payment is due for a subscription, Stripe generates an <span><span><a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/billing/invoices/subscription" tabindex="0"><span>invoice</span></a></span><span class="⚙  as-d as-6y as-6z as-h as-70 ⚙1rjyh41"></span></span> and a PaymentIntent. The PaymentIntent ID attaches to the invoice and you can access it from the Invoice and Subscription objects.</p><p>The status of the PaymentIntent affects the status of the invoice and the subscription. Here’s how the different outcomes of a payment map to the different statuses:</p><div class="Table Table--solid ⚙  as-3r as-4r as-6o as-x ⚙1yz2rkt"><div class="⚙  as-7b as-7c as-7d as-7e as-7f as-7g as-7h ⚙ld3g7k"><table class="⚙  as-x as-7i as-7j as-5o as-53 as-7k as-7l as-7m as-7n ⚙by0pj1"><thead><tr class="⚙ as-2z ⚙lu93gh"><th class="⚙ rs-3 as-7o as-7p as-7q as-12 as-7r as-3n as-3o as-54 as-7s ⚙batyg0" scope="column">Payment outcome</th><th class="⚙ rs-3 as-7o as-7p as-7q as-12 as-7r as-3n as-3o as-54 as-7s ⚙batyg0" scope="column">PaymentIntent status</th><th class="⚙ rs-3 as-7o as-7p as-7q as-12 as-7r as-3n as-3o as-54 as-7s ⚙batyg0" scope="column">Invoice status</th><th class="⚙ rs-3 as-7o as-7p as-7q as-12 as-7r as-3n as-3o as-54 as-7s ⚙batyg0" scope="column">Subscription status</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr class="⚙ as-2z ⚙lu93gh"><td class="⚙  as-7p as-7t as-l as-7u as-4n as-7v as-7w as-7x ⚙1gceb7q">Success</td><td class="⚙  as-7p as-7t as-l as-7u as-4n as-7v as-7w as-7x ⚙1gceb7q"><code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">succeeded</code></td><td class="⚙  as-7p as-7t as-l as-7u as-4n as-7v as-7w as-7x ⚙1gceb7q"><code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">paid</code></td><td class="⚙  as-7p as-7t as-l as-7u as-4n as-7v as-7w as-7x ⚙1gceb7q"><code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">active</code></td></tr><tr class="⚙ as-2z ⚙lu93gh"><td class="⚙  as-7p as-7t as-l as-7u as-4n as-7v as-7w as-7x ⚙1gceb7q">Fails because of a card error</td><td class="⚙  as-7p as-7t as-l as-7u as-4n as-7v as-7w as-7x ⚙1gceb7q"><code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">requires<!-- -->_<wbr/>payment<!-- -->_<wbr/>method</code></td><td class="⚙  as-7p as-7t as-l as-7u as-4n as-7v as-7w as-7x ⚙1gceb7q"><code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">open</code></td><td class="⚙  as-7p as-7t as-l as-7u as-4n as-7v as-7w as-7x ⚙1gceb7q"><code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">incomplete</code></td></tr><tr class="⚙ as-2z ⚙lu93gh"><td class="⚙  as-7p as-7t as-l as-7u as-4n as-7v as-7w as-7x ⚙1gceb7q">Fails because of authentication</td><td class="⚙  as-7p as-7t as-l as-7u as-4n as-7v as-7w as-7x ⚙1gceb7q"><code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">requires<!-- -->_<wbr/>action</code></td><td class="⚙  as-7p as-7t as-l as-7u as-4n as-7v as-7w as-7x ⚙1gceb7q"><code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">open</code></td><td class="⚙  as-7p as-7t as-l as-7u as-4n as-7v as-7w as-7x ⚙1gceb7q"><code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">incomplete</code></td></tr></tbody></table></div></div><h3 class="Heading Heading--anchored" id="delayed-payment-confirmation" data-toc-text="Payment methods with delayed payment confirmation"><span class="⚙ rs-4 as-w as-y as-1r ⚙w54g2d"><span class="⚙ rs-4  as-w as-y as-1r as-v ⚙1hdidop"><span class="algolia-heading-content ⚙">Payment methods with delayed payment confirmation </span></span><span class="⚙ as-3r ⚙1kc9vkf"><span role="button" class="⚙  as-3r as-1e as-1u as-1v as-6v ⚙sm5kdb"><svg data-testid="anchorIcon" aria-hidden="true" width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 16 16" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" class="⚙   as-2e as-2f as-2g as-2h as-2i as-44 as-45 as-6w as-6x ⚙1o8zlja"><path d="M12.707 7.091a2.685 2.685 0 0 0-3.798-3.798L7.763 4.44a.753.753 0 1 1-1.066-1.066l1.147-1.146a4.192 4.192 0 0 1 5.928 5.928l-1.146 1.147a.753.753 0 0 1-1.066-1.066l1.147-1.146Z"></path><path d="M5.26 10.76a.753.753 0 0 1 0-1.066l4.434-4.435a.753.753 0 0 1 1.065 1.065L6.325 10.76a.753.753 0 0 1-1.066 0Z"></path><path d="M3.293 12.707a2.685 2.685 0 0 1 0-3.798L4.44 7.763a.753.753 0 1 0-1.066-1.066L2.228 7.844a4.192 4.192 0 0 0 5.928 5.928l1.147-1.146a.753.753 0 0 0-1.066-1.066l-1.146 1.147a2.685 2.685 0 0 1-3.798 0Z"></path></svg></span></span></span></h3><p>Payment methods that can’t immediately return payment status when a customer attempts a transaction (for example, ACH Direct Debit) handle subscription status transitions differently. When you use these types of payment methods, a subscription can move directly to <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">active</code> after creation and bypass <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">incomplete</code>. If the payment fails later, Stripe voids the invoice but the subscription remains <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">active</code>. Use this behavior when you design your access control and retry logic.</p><p>The following sections explain these statuses and the actions to take for each.</p><h3 class="Heading Heading--anchored" id="succeeded" data-toc-text="Payment succeeded"><span class="⚙ rs-4 as-w as-y as-1r ⚙w54g2d"><span class="⚙ rs-4  as-w as-y as-1r as-v ⚙1hdidop"><span class="algolia-heading-content ⚙">Payment succeeded </span></span><span class="⚙ as-3r ⚙1kc9vkf"><span role="button" class="⚙  as-3r as-1e as-1u as-1v as-6v ⚙sm5kdb"><svg data-testid="anchorIcon" aria-hidden="true" width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 16 16" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" class="⚙   as-2e as-2f as-2g as-2h as-2i as-44 as-45 as-6w as-6x ⚙1o8zlja"><path d="M12.707 7.091a2.685 2.685 0 0 0-3.798-3.798L7.763 4.44a.753.753 0 1 1-1.066-1.066l1.147-1.146a4.192 4.192 0 0 1 5.928 5.928l-1.146 1.147a.753.753 0 0 1-1.066-1.066l1.147-1.146Z"></path><path d="M5.26 10.76a.753.753 0 0 1 0-1.066l4.434-4.435a.753.753 0 0 1 1.065 1.065L6.325 10.76a.753.753 0 0 1-1.066 0Z"></path><path d="M3.293 12.707a2.685 2.685 0 0 1 0-3.798L4.44 7.763a.753.753 0 1 0-1.066-1.066L2.228 7.844a4.192 4.192 0 0 0 5.928 5.928l1.147-1.146a.753.753 0 0 0-1.066-1.066l-1.146 1.147a2.685 2.685 0 0 1-3.798 0Z"></path></svg></span></span></span></h3><p>When the customer’s payment is successful:</p><ul class="List default ⚙ as-7y ⚙18x7cqb"><li class="ListItem--hasMultipleChildren ⚙">The <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">status</code> of the PaymentIntent moves to <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">succeeded</code>.</li><li class="ListItem--hasMultipleChildren ⚙">The <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">status</code> of the subscription is <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">active</code>.</li><li class="ListItem--hasMultipleChildren ⚙">The <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">status</code> of the invoice is <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">paid</code>.</li><li class="ListItem--hasMultipleChildren ⚙">Stripe sends an <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">invoice<!-- -->.<wbr/>paid</code> event to your configured webhook endpoints.</li></ul><p>For <span><span><a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/payments/payment-methods/integration-options" tabindex="0"><span>payment methods</span></a></span><span class="⚙  as-d as-6y as-6z as-h as-70 ⚙1rjyh41"></span></span> with longer processing periods, subscriptions are immediately activated. In these cases, the status of the PaymentIntent might be <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">processing</code> for an <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">active</code> subscription until the payment succeeds.</p><p>With your subscription now activated, <span><span><a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="#provision-access" tabindex="0"><span>provision access</span></a></span><span class="⚙  as-d as-6y as-6z as-h as-70 ⚙1rjyh41"></span></span> to your product.</p><h3 class="Heading Heading--anchored" id="requires-payment-method" data-toc-text="Requires payment method"><span class="⚙ rs-4 as-w as-y as-1r ⚙w54g2d"><span class="⚙ rs-4  as-w as-y as-1r as-v ⚙1hdidop"><span class="algolia-heading-content ⚙">Requires payment method </span></span><span class="⚙ as-3r ⚙1kc9vkf"><span role="button" class="⚙  as-3r as-1e as-1u as-1v as-6v ⚙sm5kdb"><svg data-testid="anchorIcon" aria-hidden="true" width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 16 16" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" class="⚙   as-2e as-2f as-2g as-2h as-2i as-44 as-45 as-6w as-6x ⚙1o8zlja"><path d="M12.707 7.091a2.685 2.685 0 0 0-3.798-3.798L7.763 4.44a.753.753 0 1 1-1.066-1.066l1.147-1.146a4.192 4.192 0 0 1 5.928 5.928l-1.146 1.147a.753.753 0 0 1-1.066-1.066l1.147-1.146Z"></path><path d="M5.26 10.76a.753.753 0 0 1 0-1.066l4.434-4.435a.753.753 0 0 1 1.065 1.065L6.325 10.76a.753.753 0 0 1-1.066 0Z"></path><path d="M3.293 12.707a2.685 2.685 0 0 1 0-3.798L4.44 7.763a.753.753 0 1 0-1.066-1.066L2.228 7.844a4.192 4.192 0 0 0 5.928 5.928l1.147-1.146a.753.753 0 0 0-1.066-1.066l-1.146 1.147a2.685 2.685 0 0 1-3.798 0Z"></path></svg></span></span></span></h3><p>If payment fails because of a <a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/api/errors#errors-card_error" tabindex="0"><span>card error</span></a> such as a <span><span><a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/declines#issuer-declines" tabindex="0"><span>decline</span></a></span><span class="⚙  as-d as-6y as-6z as-h as-70 ⚙1rjyh41"></span></span>:</p><ul class="List default ⚙ as-7y ⚙18x7cqb"><li class="ListItem--hasMultipleChildren ⚙">The <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">status</code> of the PaymentIntent is <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">requires<!-- -->_<wbr/>payment<!-- -->_<wbr/>method</code>.</li><li class="ListItem--hasMultipleChildren ⚙">The <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">status</code> of the subscription is <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">incomplete</code>.</li><li class="ListItem--hasMultipleChildren ⚙">The <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">status</code> of the invoice is <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">open</code>.</li></ul><p>To handle these scenarios:</p><ul class="List default ⚙ as-7y ⚙18x7cqb"><li class="⚙">Notify the customer.</li><li class="ListItem--hasMultipleChildren ⚙">Collect new payment information and <a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/api/payment_intents/confirm" tabindex="0"><span>confirm the PaymentIntent</span></a>.</li><li class="ListItem--hasMultipleChildren ⚙">Update the <a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/api/subscriptions/object#subscription_object-default_payment_method" tabindex="0"><span>default payment method</span></a> on the subscription.</li><li class="ListItem--hasMultipleChildren ⚙">Stripe re-attempts payment using <span><span><a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/invoicing/automatic-collection#smart-retries" tabindex="0"><span>Smart Retries</span></a></span><span class="⚙  as-d as-6y as-6z as-h as-70 ⚙1rjyh41"></span></span> or based on your custom <a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="https://dashboard.stripe.com/account/billing/automatic" tabindex="0"><span>retry rules</span></a>.</li><li class="ListItem--hasMultipleChildren ⚙">Use the <span><span><a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/billing/revenue-recovery/smart-retries#invoice-payment-failed-webhook" tabindex="0"><span>invoice.payment_failed</span></a></span><span class="⚙  as-d as-6y as-6z as-h as-70 ⚙1rjyh41"></span></span> event to monitor subscription payment failure events and retry attempt updates. After a payment attempt on an invoice, its <a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/api#invoice_object-next_payment_attempt" tabindex="0"><span>next_payment_attempt</span></a> value is set using the current subscription settings in your Dashboard.</li></ul><p>Learn how to <span><span><a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/billing/subscriptions/webhooks#payment-failures" tabindex="0"><span>handle payment failures for subscriptions</span></a></span><span class="⚙  as-d as-6y as-6z as-h as-70 ⚙1rjyh41"></span></span>.</p><h3 class="Heading Heading--anchored" id="failed-recurring-payments" data-toc-text="Handle failed recurring payments"><span class="⚙ rs-4 as-w as-y as-1r ⚙w54g2d"><span class="⚙ rs-4  as-w as-y as-1r as-v ⚙1hdidop"><span class="algolia-heading-content ⚙">Handle failed recurring payments </span></span><span class="⚙ as-3r ⚙1kc9vkf"><span role="button" class="⚙  as-3r as-1e as-1u as-1v as-6v ⚙sm5kdb"><svg data-testid="anchorIcon" aria-hidden="true" width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 16 16" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" class="⚙   as-2e as-2f as-2g as-2h as-2i as-44 as-45 as-6w as-6x ⚙1o8zlja"><path d="M12.707 7.091a2.685 2.685 0 0 0-3.798-3.798L7.763 4.44a.753.753 0 1 1-1.066-1.066l1.147-1.146a4.192 4.192 0 0 1 5.928 5.928l-1.146 1.147a.753.753 0 0 1-1.066-1.066l1.147-1.146Z"></path><path d="M5.26 10.76a.753.753 0 0 1 0-1.066l4.434-4.435a.753.753 0 0 1 1.065 1.065L6.325 10.76a.753.753 0 0 1-1.066 0Z"></path><path d="M3.293 12.707a2.685 2.685 0 0 1 0-3.798L4.44 7.763a.753.753 0 1 0-1.066-1.066L2.228 7.844a4.192 4.192 0 0 0 5.928 5.928l1.147-1.146a.753.753 0 0 0-1.066-1.066l-1.146 1.147a2.685 2.685 0 0 1-3.798 0Z"></path></svg></span></span></span></h3><p>When a recurring subscription payment fails, listen for <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">invoice<!-- -->.<wbr/>payment<!-- -->_<wbr/>failed</code>. <span><span><a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/billing/subscriptions/webhooks#state-changes" tabindex="0"><span>Monitor subscription status changes</span></a></span><span class="⚙  as-d as-6y as-6z as-h as-70 ⚙1rjyh41"></span></span> to detect transitions to <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">past<!-- -->_<wbr/>due</code> or <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">unpaid</code>. Notify the customer so they can update their payment method or pay the open invoice. <span><span><a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/webhooks#verify-events" tabindex="0"><span>Verify webhook signatures</span></a></span><span class="⚙  as-d as-6y as-6z as-h as-70 ⚙1rjyh41"></span></span> before you process events.</p><p>Configure <span><span><a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/billing/revenue-recovery/smart-retries" tabindex="0"><span>Smart Retries or custom retry rules</span></a></span><span class="⚙  as-d as-6y as-6z as-h as-70 ⚙1rjyh41"></span></span> to retry failed payments. In your <a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="https://dashboard.stripe.com/settings/billing/automatic" tabindex="0"><span>failed payment settings</span></a>, choose what happens to the subscription after the final retry. Use the <span><span><a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="#subscription-statuses" tabindex="0"><span>subscription status table</span></a></span><span class="⚙  as-d as-6y as-6z as-h as-70 ⚙1rjyh41"></span></span> to determine when to revoke access and how to reactivate <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">past<!-- -->_<wbr/>due</code> and <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">unpaid</code> subscriptions. See <span><span><a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/billing/collection-method#handle-recurring-charge-failures" tabindex="0"><span>Failed recurring charges</span></a></span><span class="⚙  as-d as-6y as-6z as-h as-70 ⚙1rjyh41"></span></span> for more details.</p><h3 class="Heading Heading--anchored" id="requires-action" data-toc-text="Requires action"><span class="⚙ rs-4 as-w as-y as-1r ⚙w54g2d"><span class="⚙ rs-4  as-w as-y as-1r as-v ⚙1hdidop"><span class="algolia-heading-content ⚙">Requires action </span></span><span class="⚙ as-3r ⚙1kc9vkf"><span role="button" class="⚙  as-3r as-1e as-1u as-1v as-6v ⚙sm5kdb"><svg data-testid="anchorIcon" aria-hidden="true" width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 16 16" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" class="⚙   as-2e as-2f as-2g as-2h as-2i as-44 as-45 as-6w as-6x ⚙1o8zlja"><path d="M12.707 7.091a2.685 2.685 0 0 0-3.798-3.798L7.763 4.44a.753.753 0 1 1-1.066-1.066l1.147-1.146a4.192 4.192 0 0 1 5.928 5.928l-1.146 1.147a.753.753 0 0 1-1.066-1.066l1.147-1.146Z"></path><path d="M5.26 10.76a.753.753 0 0 1 0-1.066l4.434-4.435a.753.753 0 0 1 1.065 1.065L6.325 10.76a.753.753 0 0 1-1.066 0Z"></path><path d="M3.293 12.707a2.685 2.685 0 0 1 0-3.798L4.44 7.763a.753.753 0 1 0-1.066-1.066L2.228 7.844a4.192 4.192 0 0 0 5.928 5.928l1.147-1.146a.753.753 0 0 0-1.066-1.066l-1.146 1.147a2.685 2.685 0 0 1-3.798 0Z"></path></svg></span></span></span></h3><p>Some payment methods require customer authentication with <span><span><a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/payments/3d-secure" tabindex="0"><span>3D Secure</span></a></span><span class="⚙  as-d as-6y as-6z as-h as-70 ⚙1rjyh41"></span></span> (3DS). Whether authentication is required depends on your <span><span><a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/payments/3d-secure/authentication-flow#three-ds-radar" tabindex="0"><span>Radar rules</span></a></span><span class="⚙  as-d as-6y as-6z as-h as-70 ⚙1rjyh41"></span></span> and the issuing bank for the card.</p><p>If payment fails because the customer needs to authenticate a payment:</p><ul class="List default ⚙ as-7y ⚙18x7cqb"><li class="ListItem--hasMultipleChildren ⚙">The <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">status</code> of the PaymentIntent is <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">requires<!-- -->_<wbr/>action</code>.</li><li class="ListItem--hasMultipleChildren ⚙">The <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">status</code> of the subscription is <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">incomplete</code>.</li><li class="ListItem--hasMultipleChildren ⚙">The <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">status</code> of the invoice is <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">open</code>.</li></ul><p>To handle these scenarios:</p><ul class="List default ⚙ as-7y ⚙18x7cqb"><li class="ListItem--hasMultipleChildren ⚙">Monitor for the <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">invoice<!-- -->.<wbr/>payment<!-- -->_<wbr/>action<!-- -->_<wbr/>required</code> event notification with <span><span><a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/billing/subscriptions/webhooks" tabindex="0"><span>webhook endpoints</span></a></span><span class="⚙  as-d as-6y as-6z as-h as-70 ⚙1rjyh41"></span></span>. This indicates that authentication is required.</li><li class="ListItem--hasMultipleChildren ⚙">Notify your customer that they must authenticate.<!-- --> <!-- -->Retrieve the PaymentIntent’s client secret and pass it to <a class="Link ⚙ rs-2 rs-3 rs-3 as-8 as-9 as-a as-b as-5l as-17 as-18 as-19 as-1a as-1b as-1c as-1d as-1e as-1f as-1g as-1h ⚙3jnnh" href="/js/payment_intents/handle_next_action" tabindex="0"><span>stripe.handleNextAction</span></a>. This guides the customer through any required steps and returns the result to your application.</li><li class="ListItem--hasMultipleChildren ⚙">Monitor the <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">invoice<!-- -->.<wbr/>paid</code> event on your event destination to verify that the payment succeeded. Users can leave your application before <code class="InlineCode ⚙ rs-3 as-67 as-2i as-5e as-71 as-c as-1j as-3p as-4o as-72 as-1w as-1x as-3e as-3i ⚙2edb9o">handleNextAction()</code> finishes. Verifying whether the payment succeeded allows you to correctly provision your product.</li></ul><div class="SeeAlso Box-root"><h2 class="Heading Heading--anchored" id="see-also" data-toc-text="See also"><span class="⚙ rs-4 as-w as-y as-1r ⚙w54g2d"><span class="⚙ rs-4  as-w as-y as-1r as-v ⚙1hdidop"><span class="algolia-heading-content ⚙">See also</span></span><span class="⚙ as-3r let customers make recurring payments to access a product or service. When you create a subscription, Stripe automatically generates invoices, attempts payment collection, and manages the subscription status throughout its lifecycle. A subscription moves through a predictable set of states, from creation to cancellation."]},{"\u0024\u0024mdtype":"Tag","name":"Paragraph","attributes":{},"children":["Unlike one-time payments, subscriptions require storing customer and payment method information for future billing cycles. Stripe handles the payment retry logic, dunning, and status transitions."]},{"\u0024\u0024mdtype":"Tag","name":"Heading","attributes":{"id":"subscription-lifecycle","level":2,"anchored":true,"toc":"Subscription lifecycle "},"children":["Subscription lifecycle "]},{"\u0024\u0024mdtype":"Tag","name":"Paragraph","attributes":{},"children":["Each of the following subscription lifecycle phases maps to a status change on the ",{"\u0024\u0024mdtype":"Tag","name":"Link","attributes":{"href":"\u002Fapi\u002Fsubscriptions\u002Fobject"},"children":["Subscription object"]},". Understanding these statuses helps you know when to provision access, notify customers, and handle errors. You can use ",{"\u0024\u0024mdtype":"Tag","name":"Link","attributes":{"href":"\u002Fbilling\u002Fsubscriptions\u002Fwebhooks#state-changes"},"children":["webhook events"]}," to monitor and handle transitions between statuses."]},{"\u0024\u0024mdtype":"Tag","name":"Heading","attributes":{"level":3,"anchored":true,"toc":"Create the subscription","id":"create-the-subscription"},"children":["Create the subscription"]},{"\u0024\u0024mdtype":"Tag","name":"Paragraph","attributes":{},"children":["Create a new subscription in the ",{"\u0024\u0024mdtype":"Tag","name":"Link","attributes":{"href":"https:\u002F\u002Fdashboard.stripe.com\u002Fsubscriptions?status=active"},"children":["Dashboard"]}," or with the ",{"\u0024\u0024mdtype":"Tag","name":"Link","attributes":{"href":"\u002Fapi\u002Fsubscriptions\u002Fcreate"},"children":["Subscriptions API"]},". The resulting ",{"\u0024\u0024mdtype":"Tag","name":"Link","attributes":{"href":"\u002Fapi\u002Fsubscriptions\u002Fobject"},"children":["Subscription object"]}," contains the subscribed customer, ",{"\u0024\u0024mdtype":"Tag","name":"Link","attributes":{"href":"\u002Fapi\u002Fproducts"},"children":["product"]},", and ",{"\u0024\u0024mdtype":"Tag","name":"Link","attributes":{"href":"\u002Fapi\u002Fprices\u002Fobject"},"children":["price"]},", and the ",{"\u0024\u0024mdtype":"Tag","name":"InlineCode","attributes":{"content":"status","\u0024\u0024markdoc:type":"node"},"children":[]}," reflecting the subscription’s current lifecycle state."]},{"\u0024\u0024mdtype":"Tag","name":"Paragraph","attributes":{},"children":["When you create a subscription that requires an immediate payment, Stripe also creates an ",{"\u0024\u0024mdtype":"Tag","name":"Link","attributes":{"href":"\u002Fbilling\u002Finvoices\u002Fsubscription"},"children":["Invoice"]}," and a ",{"\u0024\u0024mdtype":"Tag","name":"Link","attributes":{"href":"\u002Fpayments\u002Fpayment-intents"},"children":["PaymentIntent"]},".
